const API='';let token=localStorage.getItem('xv_token');let me=null;const $=s=>document.querySelector(s);const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));
async function api(path,opt={}){const r=await fetch(API+path,{...opt,headers:{'content-type':'application/json',...(opt.headers||{}),...(token?{authorization:'Bearer '+token}:{})}});if(!r.ok){let e;try{e=await r.json()}catch{};throw new Error(e?.error||`HTTP ${r.status}`)}return r.headers.get('content-type')?.includes('application/json')?r.json():r.blob()}
function toast(m){const t=$('#toast');if(t){t.textContent=m;t.style.display='block';setTimeout(()=>t.style.display='none',2500)}}
async function login(e){e.preventDefault();try{const b=Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.currentTarget));const x=await api('/api/auth/login',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(b)});token=x.token;localStorage.setItem('xv_token',token);location.reload()}catch(err){toast(err.message)}}
function logout(){localStorage.removeItem('xv_token');location.href='/admin.html'}
async function boot(){if(!token)return;try{const m=await api('/api/me');me=m.user;window.impersonation=m.impersonation;render()}catch{logout()}}
const menu=[['VisÃ£o Geral','home'],['CatÃ¡logo','products'],['Tabelas de preÃ§o','prices'],['Estoque Inteligente','stock'],['Clientes','clients'],['Pedidos','orders'],['Vendedores','users'],['LogÃ­stica','logistics'],['RelatÃ³rios','reports'],['Auditoria','audit'],['UsuÃ¡rios','users'],['ConfiguraÃ§Ãµes','settings'],['IA Administrativa','ai']];

async function render(){document.body.innerHTML=shell();document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>show(b.dataset.view));await show(location.hash.slice(1)||'home')}
async function show(view){document.querySelectorAll('[data-view]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.view===view));$('#title').textContent=({home:'VisÃ£o Geral',products:'CatÃ¡logo',stock:'Estoque Inteligente',clients:'Clientes',orders:'Pedidos',users:'UsuÃ¡rios e Vendedores',logistics:'LogÃ­stica',reports:'RelatÃ³rios',audit:'Auditoria',settings:'ConfiguraÃ§Ãµes',prices:'Tabelas de preÃ§o',ai:'IA Administrativa'})[view]||view;location.hash=view;const v=$('#view');v.innerHTML='<div class="panel">Carregando...</div>';try{if(view==='home')return home(v);if(view==='products')return products(v);if(view==='prices')return prices(v);if(view==='stock')return stock(v);if(view==='clients')return clients(v);if(view==='orders')return orders(v);if(view==='users')return users(v);if(view==='logistics')return logistics(v);if(view==='reports')return reports(v);if(view==='audit')return auditView(v);if(view==='settings')return settings(v);if(view==='ai')return ai(v)}catch(e){v.innerHTML=`<div class="panel">Erro: ${esc(e.message)}</div>`}}

async function products(v){const [p,c]=await Promise.all([api('/api/products'),api('/api/categories')]);v.innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><div class="actions"><input id="pq" class="search" placeholder="Pesquisar produto por nome, SKU ou cÃ³digo de barras"><button class="btn primary" onclick="newProduct()">+ Produto</button></div></div><div class="tablewrap"><table class="table"><thead><tr><th>Produto</th><th>SKU</th><th>Categoria</th><th>PreÃ§o</th><th>Estoque</th><th>AÃ§Ãµes</th></tr></thead><tbody>${p.items.map(x=>`<tr><td>${esc(x.nome)}</td><td>${esc(x.sku)}</td><td>${esc(x.categoria_nome||'-')}</td><td>R$ ${Number(x.preco_base).toFixed(2)}</td><td>${x.quantidade_disponivel}</td><td><button class="btn" onclick='editProduct(${JSON.stringify(x)})'>Editar</button> <button class="btn" onclick="adjustStock('${x.id}')">Estoque</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`}
function newProduct(){modal('Novo produto',`<form id="productForm" class="form"><div class="field"><label>Nome</label><input name="nome" required></div><div class="field"><label>SKU</label><input name="sku" required></div><div class="field"><label>CÃ³digo de barras</label><input name="codigo_barras"></div><div class="field"><label>Marca</label><input name="marca"></div><div class="field"><label>Sabor</label><input name="sabor"></div><div class="field"><label>Tamanho</label><input name="tamanho"></div><div class="field"><label>PreÃ§o base</label><input name="preco_base" type="number" step="0.01"></div><div class="field"><label>Quantidade inicial</label><input name="quantidade" type="number" value="0"></div><div class="field"><label>Estoque mÃ­nimo</label><input name="estoque_minimo" type="number" value="0"></div><div class="actions"><button class="btn primary">Salvar</button></div></form>`);$('#productForm').onsubmit=async e=>{e.preventDefault();try{await api('/api/products',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.currentTarget)))});closeModal();toast('Produto criado');show('products')}catch(x){toast(x.message)}}}
function editProduct(x){modal('Editar produto',`<form id="productForm" class="form"><div class="field"><label>Nome</label><input name="nome" value="${esc(x.nome)}"></div><div class="field"><label>SKU</label><input name="sku" value="${esc(x.sku)}"></div><div class="field"><label>PreÃ§o</label><input name="preco_base" type="number" step="0.01" value="${x.preco_base}"></div><div class="field"><label>Foto URL</label><input name="foto_url" value="${esc(x.foto_url||'')}"></div><div class="actions"><button class="btn primary">Salvar</button></div></form>`);$('#productForm').onsubmit=async e=>{e.preventDefault();try{await api('/api/products/'+x.id,{method:'PUT',body:JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.currentTarget)))});closeModal();toast('Produto atualizado');show('products')}catch(z){toast(z.message)}}}
async function stock(v){const p=await api('/api/stock');v.innerHTML=`<div class="panel"><input class="search" placeholder="Pesquisar" oninput="filterTable(this,'stocktable')"></div><div id="stocktable" class="tablewrap"><table class="table"><thead><tr><th>Produto</th><th>SKU</th><th>DisponÃ­vel</th><th>Reservado</th><th>MÃ­nimo</th><th>Status</th><th></th></tr></thead><tbody>${p.items.map(x=>`<tr><td>${esc(x.nome)}</td><td>${esc(x.sku)}</td><td>${x.quantidade_disponivel}</td><td>${x.quantidade_reservada}</td><td>${x.estoque_minimo}</td><td class="${x.status==='OK'?'ok':'critical'}">${x.status}</td><td><button class="btn" onclick="adjustStock('${x.id}')">Ajustar</button></td></tr>`).join('')}</tbody></table></div>`}

const API='';
let token=localStorage.getItem('xv_token'),me=null;
const $=s=>document.querySelector(s);
const esc=s=>String(s??'').replace(/[&<>"']/g,m=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]));
async function api(path,opt={}){const r=await fetch(API+path,{...opt,headers:{'content-type':'application/json',...(token?{authorization:'Bearer '+token}:{})}});if(!r.ok){let e;try{e=await r.json()}catch{};throw new Error(e?.error||`HTTP ${r.status}`)}return r.headers.get('content-type')?.includes('application/json')?r.json():r.blob()}
function money(v){return `R$ ${Number(v||0).toFixed(2).replace('.',',')}`}
function logout(){localStorage.removeItem('xv_token');location.reload()}
function loginPage(){document.body.innerHTML=`<div class="login"><div class="loginbox"><img class="xv-logo" src="/assets/brand/logo-online.jpg" alt="Xiquim Vargas"><h1>${esc(document.body.dataset.role)}</h1><p>Entre com suas credenciais para acessar sua operaÃ§Ã£o.</p><form onsubmit="doLogin(event)"><div class="field"><label>Email</label><input name="email" type="email" autocomplete="username" required></div><br><div class="field"><label>Senha</label><input name="password" type="password" autocomplete="current-password" required></div><br><button class="btn primary" style="width:100%;padding:13px">Entrar na operaÃ§Ã£o</button></form></div></div>`}
async function doLogin(e){e.preventDefault();try{const x=await api('/api/auth/login',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify(Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e)))});token=x.token;localStorage.setItem('xv_token',token);location.reload()}catch(e){alert(e.message)}}
async function initRole(){if(!token)return loginPage();try{me=(await api('/api/me')).user;await renderRole()}catch{localStorage.removeItem('xv_token');loginPage()}}
function mobileHeader(title,subtitle,logo='/assets/brand/logo-online.jpg',dark=false){return `<header class="mobile-header"><div class="mobile-header-row"><img class="xv-logo" src="${logo}" alt="Xiquim Vargas"><button class="btn" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.1);border-color:rgba(255,255,255,.25);color:#fff" onclick="logout()">â‡¥</button></div><div style="margin-top:8px"><strong style="font-size:21px">${title}</strong><div class="mobile-user">${subtitle}</div></div></header>`}
function bottom(active){return `<nav class="bottom-nav"><button class="${active==='home'?'active':''}" onclick="roleHome()"><span class="ico">âŒ‚</span>InÃ­cio</button><button class="${active==='clients'?'active':''}" onclick="seller()"><span class="ico">â™™</span>Clientes</button><button class="${active==='orders'?'active':''}" onclick="sellerOrders()"><span class="ico">â–£</span>Pedidos</button><button><span class="ico">â˜°</span>Mais</button></nav>`}
async function renderRole(){const role=document.body.dataset.role;const expected={'Vendedor Externo':'seller','Frente de Loja / PDV':'cashier','Loja Online':'web_client','Motorista':'driver'}[role];if(expected&&me.role!==expected){localStorage.removeItem('xv_token');alert('Esta interface nÃ£o corresponde ao seu perfil de acesso.');return loginPage()}
if(role==='Vendedor Externo')return seller();if(role==='Frente de Loja / PDV')return pdv();if(role==='Loja Online')return loja();return driver()}

async function sellerNewOrder(client){if(!client)return alert('Selecione um cliente na carteira.');const p=await api('/api/products');window.sellerProducts=p.items;document.body.innerHTML=`<div class="mobile-shell"><div class="mobile-content">${mobileHeader('Novo pedido',`${esc(client.razao_social)} Â· Vendedor externo`)}<div class="mobile-nav" style="margin-top:15px"><button class="btn" onclick="seller()">â† Voltar</button><button class="btn" onclick="sellerOrders()">Pedidos</button></div><input class="search" style="background:#fff;color:#172033;border-color:#dbe3ec" placeholder="Pesquisar produto..." oninput="sellerFilter(this)"><div id="sellerProducts" class="product-grid" style="margin-top:12px"></div><div class="mobile-card" style="margin-top:12px;position:sticky;bottom:75px"><div class="actions"><strong>${esc(client.razao_social)}</strong><span class="muted">Reserva de estoque feita pelo servidor ao emitir</span></div><button class="btn primary" style="width:100%;margin-top:10px" onclick="submitSellerOrder('${client.id}')">Emitir pedido e gerar PDF</button></div></div></div>`;sellerRender(p.items)}
function sellerRender(items){$('#sellerProducts').innerHTML=items.map(p=>`<article class="product-card"><div class="product-thumb">${esc(p.marca||'PRODUTO')}</div><h4>${esc(p.nome)}</h4><div class="muted">${esc(p.tamanho||p.sabor||p.sku||'')}</div><div class="product-price">${money(p.preco_base)}</div><div class="muted" style="margin-bottom:8px">Estoque: ${p.quantidade_disponivel}</div><input data-p="${p.id}" type="number" min="0" max="${p.quantidade_disponivel}" value="0" class="search" style="background:#f7f9fc;color:#172033"></article>`).join('')}
function sellerFilter(i){sellerRender(window.sellerProducts.filter(x=>(x.nome+' '+x.sku+' '+(x.marca||'')).toLowerCase().includes(i.value.toLowerCase())))}
async function submitSellerOrder(clientId){const itens=[...document.querySelectorAll('[data-p]')].map(x=>({produto_id:x.dataset.p,quantidade:Number(x.value)})).filter(x=>x.quantidade>0);if(!itens.length)return alert('Adicione pelo menos um produto.');try{const x=await api('/api/orders',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({cliente_id:clientId,itens,origem:'VENDEDOR'})});alert('Pedido '+x.item.numero+' criado e PDF arquivado.');sellerOrders()}catch(e){alert(e.message)}}

async function pdv(){const [c,p]=await Promise.all([api('/api/clients'),api('/api/products')]);window.pdvProducts=p.items;window.pdvCart={};document.body.innerHTML=`<div class="pdv-shell dark-pdv"><header class="pdv-top"><img class="xv-logo" src="/assets/brand/logo-pdv.jpg" alt="Xiquim Vargas Frente de Loja"><div class="pdv-title">PDV â€“ Frente de Loja</div><div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:14px;color:#b7c2cf"><span>â—· ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString('pt-BR',{hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'})}</span><span>${esc(me.nome)}</span><button class="btn" onclick="logout()">â‡¥</button></div></header><main class="pdv-layout"><section class="pdv-left"><input id="pdvSearch" class="pdv-search" placeholder="â–¥  CÃ³digo de barras ou nome do produto... function pdvFilter(i){const q=i.value.toLowerCase();const items=window.pdvProducts.filter(x=>(x.nome+' '+x.sku+' '+(x.codigo_barras||'')).toLowerCase().includes(q));$('#pdvCount').textContent=items.length+' resultado(s)';pdvRender(items)}
function pdvRender(items){$('#pdvProducts').innerHTML=items.slice(0,40).map(p=>`<article class="pdv-product" onclick="pdvAdd('${p.id}')"><div class="pdv-thumb">${esc(p.marca||'')}</div><div><h4>${esc(p.nome)}</h4><small>${esc(p.tamanho||'')} Â· Estoque: ${p.quantidade_disponivel} un</small><br><small>CÃ³d: ${esc(p.codigo_barras||p.sku||'-')}</small></div><div class="price">${money(p.preco_base)}</div></article>`).join('')}
function pdvAdd(id){const p=window.pdvProducts.find(x=>x.id===id);if(!p)return;window.pdvCart[id]=(window.pdvCart[id]||0)+1;if(window.pdvCart[id]>p.quantidade_disponivel)window.pdvCart[id]=p.quantidade_disponivel;pdvCartRender()}
function pdvClear(){window.pdvCart={};pdvCartRender()}
function pdvCartRender(){const rows=Object.entries(window.pdvCart).filter(([,q])=>q>0).map(([id,q],i)=>{const p=window.pdvProducts.find(x=>x.id===id),total=q*Number(p.preco_base);return `<div class="pdv-row"><span class="num">${String(i+1).padStart(2,'0')}</span><div><strong>${esc(p.nome)}</strong><br><small style="color:#9aa8b7">CÃ³d: ${esc(p.codigo_barras||p.sku)}</small></div><div class="qty-control"><button onclick="pdvQty('${id}',-1)">âˆ’</button><span>${q}</span><button onclick="pdvQty('${id}',1)">ï¼‹</button></div><strong style="color:var(--xv-orange)">${money(total)}</strong><button class="btn" onclick="delete window.pdvCart['${id}'];pdvCartRender()">âŒ«</button></div>`}).join('');$('#pdvOrderList').innerHTML=rows||'<div style="padding:30px;text-align:center;color:#718198">Adicione produtos ao pedido usando a lista ao lado.</div>';const total=Object.entries(window.pdvCart).reduce((s,[id,q])=>s+q*Number(window.pdvProducts.find(x=>x.id===id).preco_base),0);const units=Object.values(window.pdvCart).reduce((s,x)=>s+x,0);$('#pdvTotal').textContent=money(total);$('#pdvUnits').textContent=units+' (ns)'}
function pdvQty(id,delta){const p=window.pdvProducts.find(x=>x.id===id);window.pdvCart[id]=Math.max(0,Math.min(p.quantidade_disponivel,(window.pdvCart[id]||0)+delta));pdvCartRender()}
async function submitPDV(){const itens=Object.entries(window.pdvCart).filter(([,q])=>q>0).map(([produto_id,quantidade])=>({produto_id,quantidade}));if(!itens.length)return alert('O pedido estÃ¡ vazio.');if(!$('#pdvClient').value)return alert('Selecione o cliente.');try{const x=await api('/api/orders',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({cliente_id:$('#pdvClient').value,itens,origem:'BALCAO'})});alert('Pedido '+x.item.numero+' criado. PDF gerado e arquivado.');pdv()}catch(e){alert(e.message)}}

async function driver(){const [r,o]=await Promise.all([api('/api/logistics/routes'),api('/api/orders')]);const byId=new Map(o.items.map(x=>[x.id,x]));const stops=r.items.flatMap(route=>{let ids=[];try{ids=JSON.parse(route.sequencia_json)}catch{};return ids.map((id,i)=>({id,order:byId.get(id),route,i}))}).filter(x=>x.order);document.body.innerHTML=`<div class="mobile-shell mobile-dark"><div class="mobile-content">${mobileHeader('Fila do dia',`${esc(me.nome)} Â· LogÃ­stica interna`,'/assets/brand/logo-admin.jpg',true)}<div class="mobile-nav" style="margin-top:15px"><button class="btn primary" onclick="driver()">â†» Atualizar fila</button><button class="btn" onclick="logout()">Sair</button></div><div class="mobile-card" style="margin-bottom:12px"><div class="route-status">OperaÃ§Ã£o de entrega</div><h2 style="margin:5px 0">${stops.length} parada(s) na fila</h2><div class="muted">A sequÃªncia foi definida pela Torre de Controle. Abra o mapa, entregue e conclua.</div></div>${stops.map((s,idx)=>{const address=s.order.endereco||s.order.cliente_endereco||'EndereÃ§o nÃ£o informado';return `<article class="route-card"><div class="route-row"><div class="route-number">${idx+1}</div><div style="flex:1"><div class="route-status">Parada ${idx+1}</div><h3 style="margin:4px 0;color:#14203a">${esc(s.order.cliente_nome)}</h3><div class="muted">${esc(address)}</div><div class="muted" style="margin-top:5px">Pedido ${esc(s.order.numero)} Â· ${money(s.order.total)}</div></div></div><div class="route-actions"><a class="btn" target="_blank" href="https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${encodeURIComponent(address)}">âŒ– Abrir Maps</a><button class="btn good" onclick="completeDriver('${s.id}')">âœ“ ConcluÃ­do</button></div></article>`}).join('')}</div></div>`}

===== scripts\qa_50_loops_pro.mjs =====

import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';

const sleep=ms=>new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,ms));
const req=async(p,o={})=>{const r=await fetch(BASE+p,o);const t=await r.text();let d;try{d=JSON.parse(t)}catch{d=t}return{s:r.status,d}};

const seller=await login('vendedor@xiquimvargas.local','Vendedor@123');
const cash=await login('caixa@xiquimvargas.local','Caixa@123');
const driver=await login('motorista@xiquimvargas.local','Motorista@123');
const web=await login('cliente@xiquimvargas.local','Cliente@123');
const clients=(await auth(seller,'/api/clients')).d.items;A(clients.length===1,'seller carteira seed');
const client=clients[0];
const products=(await auth(seller,'/api/products?limit=100')).d.items;const product=products.find(x=>x.quantidade_disponivel>=60);A(product,'produto com estoque');
const did=(await auth(admin,'/api/users')).d.items.find(x=>x.role==='driver').id;
const beforeStock=db().prepare('SELECT * FROM estoque WHERE produto_id=?').get(product.id);db().close();
for(let i=1;i<=50;i++){
  A((await req('/health')).s===200,'health');
  for(const [name,t] of [['seller',seller],['cash',cash],['driver',driver],['web',web]]) A((await auth(t,'/api/dashboard')).s===403,`RBAC ${name}`);
  const page=(await auth(seller,'/api/products?page=1&limit=2')).d;A(page.items.length<=2&&page.total>=4,'pagination');
  A((await auth(seller,`/api/stock/${product.id}/movements`)).s===200,'stock movements');
  const before=Number((await auth(admin,'/api/stock')).d.items.find(x=>x.id===product.id).quantidade_disponivel);
  const o=(await auth(seller,'/api/orders',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({cliente_id:client.id,itens:[{produto_id:product.id,quantidade:1}],origem:'BALCAO',preco:0,observacoes:`loop-${i}`})}));
  A(o.s===201,`create order ${i}`);const oid=o.d.item.id;A(o.d.item.origem==='VENDEDOR','seller origin enforcement');A(Math.abs(Number(o.d.item.total)-Number(product.preco_base))<0.001,'server price authority');A(o.d.item.pdf_path,'pdf generated');
  A((await auth(seller,`/api/orders/${oid}/status`,{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({status:'ROTA'})})).s===403,'seller cannot transition');
  A((await auth(admin,`/api/orders/${oid}/status`,{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({status:'ROTA'})})).s===409,'status bypass blocked');
  A((await auth(admin,`/api/logistics/orders/${oid}/proof`,{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({data_url:'data:image/jpeg;base64,/9j/4AAQ'})})).s===409,'proof before route blocked');
  A((await auth(admin,`/api/orders/${oid}/status`,{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({status:'SEPARACAO'})})).s===200,'separation');
  const route=(await auth(admin,'/api/logistics/routes',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({pedido_ids:[oid],motorista_id:did})}));A(route.s===201,`route ${i}`);
  A((await auth(admin,'/api/logistics/routes',{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({pedido_ids:[oid],motorista_id:did})})).s===409,'duplicate route');
  A((await auth(driver,`/api/logistics/orders/${oid}/proof`,{method:'POST',body:JSON.stringify({data_url:'data:image/jpeg;base64,/9j/4AAQ'})})).s===201,'proof');
  A((await auth(driver,`/api/logistics/orders/${oid}/complete`,{method:'POST',body:'{}'})).s===200,'complete');
  A((await auth(driver,`/api/logistics/orders/${oid}/complete`,{method:'POST',body:'{}'})).s===409,'double complete');
  A((await auth(seller,`/api/orders/${oid}/pdf`)).s===200,'pdf access');
  A((await req('/storage/../src/db.js')).s!==200,'path traversal');
  const afterStock=Number((await auth(admin,'/api/stock')).d.items.find(x=>x.id===product.id).quantidade_disponivel);A(afterStock===before-1,`stock decrement ${i}`);
  const d=db();A(d.prepare('PRAGMA integrity_check').get().integrity_check==='ok','integrity');A(d.prepare('SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM audit_logs WHERE entity_id=?').get(oid).n>=4,'audit');d.close();
  console.log(`HARDENING LOOP ${i}/50 PASS`);
  await sleep(12000);
}
const d=db();const finalStock=d.prepare('SELECT quantidade_disponivel,quantidade_reservada FROM estoque WHERE produto_id=?').get(product.id);A(finalStock.quantidade_disponivel>=0&&finalStock.quantidade_reservada===0,'final stock');d.close();
console.log('HARDENING 50/50 COMPLETE');

===== src\auth.js =====

import crypto from 'node:crypto';
const SECRET=process.env.JWT_SECRET||'dev-xiquim-vargas-change-me';
if(process.env.NODE_ENV==='production' && SECRET==='dev-xiquim-vargas-change-me') throw new Error('JWT_SECRET inseguro em produÃ§Ã£o');
const b64=o=>Buffer.from(o).toString('base64url');
export function hashPassword(password){const salt=crypto.randomBytes(16);const hash=crypto.scryptSync(password,salt,64);return `scrypt$${salt.toString('base64url')}$${hash.toString('base64url')}`}
export function verifyPassword(password,stored){const [,s,h]=stored.split('$');if(!s||!h)return false;const got=crypto.scryptSync(password,Buffer.from(s,'base64url'),64);return crypto.timingSafeEqual(got,Buffer.from(h,'base64url'))}
export function sign(payload,ttl=86400){const header=b64(JSON.stringify({alg:'HS256',typ:'JWT'}));const body=b64(JSON.stringify({...payload,iat:Math.floor(Date.now()/1000),exp:Math.floor(Date.now()/1000)+ttl}));const sig=crypto.createHmac('sha256',SECRET).update(`${header}.${body}`).digest('base64url');return `${header}.${body}.${sig}`}
export function verify(token){const [h,b,s]=String(token||'').split('.');if(!h||!b||!s||h.length>100||b.length>10000||s.length>100)throw new Error('token');const sig=crypto.createHmac('sha256',SECRET).update(`${h}.${b}`).digest('base64url');const a=Buffer.from(sig),bb=Buffer.from(s);if(a.length!==bb.length||!crypto.timingSafeEqual(a,bb))throw new Error('signature');const p=JSON.parse(Buffer.from(b,'base64url'));if(p.exp<Date.now()/1000)throw new Error('expired');return p}
export function bearer(req){const x=req.headers.authorization||'';return x.startsWith('Bearer ')?x.slice(7):null}
export function auth(req){const t=bearer(req);if(!t)throw Object.assign(new Error('AutenticaÃ§Ã£o necessÃ¡ria'),{status:401});try{return verify(t)}catch{throw Object.assign(new Error('SessÃ£o invÃ¡lida ou expirada'),{status:401})}}
export function requireRole(req,roles){const u=auth(req);if(!roles.includes(u.role))throw Object.assign(new Error('Acesso negado'),{status:403});return u}


===== src\db.js =====

import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import path from 'node:path';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';

export const ROOT = path.resolve(process.cwd());
export const STORAGE = path.join(ROOT,'storage');
fs.mkdirSync(STORAGE,{recursive:true}); fs.mkdirSync(path.join(STORAGE,'pdfs'),{recursive:true}); fs.mkdirSync(path.join(STORAGE,'proofs'),{recursive:true});
export const db = new DatabaseSync(path.join(STORAGE,'xiquim-vargas.db'));
db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON; PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;`);

db.exec(`
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS usuarios(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,nome TEXT NOT NULL,email TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,senha_hash TEXT NOT NULL,role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(role IN ('admin','seller','cashier','driver','web_client')),ativo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,telefone TEXT,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS categorias(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,nome TEXT NOT NULL,slug TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,descricao TEXT,ordem INTEGER DEFAULT 0,ativo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subcategorias(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,categoria_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES categorias(id),nome TEXT NOT NULL,slug TEXT NOT NULL,ordem INTEGER DEFAULT 0,ativo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,UNIQUE(categoria_id,slug));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS produtos(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,nome TEXT NOT NULL,sku TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,codigo_barras TEXT UNIQUE,marca TEXT,sabor TEXT,tamanho TEXT,unidade TEXT,descricao TEXT,foto_url TEXT,categoria_id TEXT REFERENCES categorias(id),subcategoria_id TEXT REFERENCES subcategorias(id),preco_base REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,ativo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS estoque(produto_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES produtos(id),quantidade_disponivel INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,quantidade_reservada INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,estoque_minimo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS movimentacoes_estoque(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,produto_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES produtos(id),tipo TEXT NOT NULL,quantidade INTEGER NOT NULL,quantidade_anterior INTEGER NOT NULL,quantidade_posterior INTEGER NOT NULL,operador_id TEXT REFERENCES usuarios(id),observacao TEXT,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tabelas_preco(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,nome TEXT NOT NULL,ativo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tabela_preco_itens(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,tabela_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES tabelas_preco(id),produto_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES produtos(id),preco REAL NOT NULL,UNIQUE(tabela_id,produto_id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS clientes(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,razao_social TEXT NOT NULL,nome_fantasia TEXT,cnpj_cpf TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,email TEXT,telefone TEXT,endereco TEXT NOT NULL,cidade TEXT,estado TEXT,cep TEXT,latitude REAL,longitude REAL,limite_credito REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,tabela_preco_id TEXT REFERENCES tabelas_preco(id),vendedor_id TEXT REFERENCES usuarios(id),ativo INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 1,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pedidos(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,numero TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL,cliente_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES clientes(id),operador_id TEXT REFERENCES usuarios(id),origem TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(origem IN ('WEB','VENDEDOR','BALCAO')),status TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(status IN ('PENDENTE','SEPARACAO','ROTA','CONCLUIDO')),total REAL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,pdf_path TEXT,observacoes TEXT,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS pedido_itens(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,pedido_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES pedidos(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,produto_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES produtos(id),quantidade INTEGER NOT NULL,preco_unitario_aplicado REAL NOT NULL,subtotal REAL NOT NULL);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rotas_logistica(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,motorista_id TEXT REFERENCES usuarios(id),data TEXT NOT NULL,status TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PLANEJADA',sequencia_json TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT '[]',distance_km REAL,estimated_minutes INTEGER,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS rota_pedidos(rota_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES rotas_logistica(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,pedido_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES pedidos(id),ordem INTEGER NOT NULL,PRIMARY KEY(rota_id,pedido_id));
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS comprovantes_entrega(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,pedido_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES pedidos(id),motorista_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES usuarios(id),arquivo_path TEXT NOT NULL,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit_logs(id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,user_id TEXT REFERENCES usuarios(id),action TEXT NOT NULL,entity TEXT NOT NULL,entity_id TEXT,before_json TEXT,after_json TEXT,ip TEXT,user_agent TEXT,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS configuracoes(chave TEXT PRIMARY KEY,valor TEXT NOT NULL,updated_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP); CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS usuario_clientes(usuario_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY REFERENCES usuarios(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,cliente_id TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL REFERENCES clientes(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,created_at TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT CURRENT_TIMESTAMP);
CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_produtos_nome ON produtos(nome); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_produtos_barcode ON produtos(codigo_barras); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clientes_doc ON clientes(cnpj_cpf); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pedidos_status ON pedidos(status); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pedidos_cliente ON pedidos(cliente_id); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pedidos_operador ON pedidos(operador_id); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_itens_produto ON pedido_itens(produto_id); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_audit_entity ON audit_logs(entity,entity_id); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_pedidos_created ON pedidos(created_at); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_produtos_categoria ON produtos(categoria_id,subcategoria_id); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_mov_estoque_produto ON movimentacoes_estoque(produto_id,created_at); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_rota_pedido ON rota_pedidos(pedido_id); CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_clientes_vendedor ON clientes(vendedor_id);
`);

export const id=()=>crypto.randomUUID();
export const now=()=>new Date().toISOString();
export function one(sql,params=[]){return db.prepare(sql).get(...params)??null}
export function all(sql,params=[]){return db.prepare(sql).all(...params)}
export function run(sql,params=[]){return db.prepare(sql).run(...params)}
export function tx(fn){db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');try{const v=fn();db.exec('COMMIT');return v}catch(e){try{db.exec('ROLLBACK')}catch{};throw e}}
export function audit({userId,action,entity,entityId,before=null,after=null,ip='',ua=''}){run(`INSERT INTO audit_logs(id,user_id,action,entity,entity_id,before_json,after_json,ip,user_agent) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)`,[id(),userId||null,action,entity,entityId||null,before?JSON.stringify(before):null,after?JSON.stringify(after):null,ip,ua])}


===== src\pdf.js =====

import fs from 'node:fs';
function esc(s){return String(s??'').replace(/\\/g,'\\\\').replace(/\(/g,'\\(').replace(/\)/g,'\\)').replace(/[^\x20-\x7E]/g,'?')}
function wrap(s,n=82){const words=String(s??'').split(/\s+/);const out=[];let line='';for(const w of words){if((line+' '+w).trim().length>n){if(line)out.push(line);line=w}else line=(line+' '+w).trim()}if(line)out.push(line);return out.length?out:['']}
export function makeOrderPdf(order,items,file){
 const lines=[`XIQUIM VARGAS â€” PEDIDO ${order.numero}`,`Data: ${new Date(order.created_at).toLocaleString('pt-BR')}`,`Cliente: ${order.cliente_nome}`,`Documento: ${order.cnpj_cpf}`,`Origem: ${order.origem_label}`,`Status: ${order.status}`,'','ITENS'];
 for(const x of items) lines.push(...wrap(`${x.quantidade} x ${x.produto_nome} @ R$ ${Number(x.preco_unitario_aplicado).toFixed(2)} = R$ ${Number(x.subtotal).toFixed(2)}`));
 lines.push('','TOTAL: R$ '+Number(order.total).toFixed(2));
 const pages=[];let page=[];for(const l of lines){for(const part of wrap(l,88)){if(page.length>=39){pages.push(page);page=[]}page.push(part)}}if(page.length)pages.push(page);
 const objs=[];const add=x=>{objs.push(x);return objs.length};const font=add('<< /Type /Font /Subtype /Type1 /BaseFont /Helvetica >>');const pageRefs=[];const contentRefs=[];
 for(const pg of pages){let content=['BT','/F1 10 Tf','50 800 Td'];pg.forEach((l,i)=>{if(i)content.push('0 -18 Td');content.push(`(${esc(l)}) Tj`)});content.push('ET');const stream=content.join('\n');contentRefs.push(add(`<< /Length ${Buffer.byteLength(stream)} >>\nstream\n${stream}\nendstream`));pageRefs.push(add('PLACEHOLDER'))}
 const pagesObj=objs.length+1;const catalogObj=pagesObj+1;for(let i=0;i<pageRefs.length;i++){objs[pageRefs[i]-1]=`<< /Type /Page /Parent ${pagesObj} 0 R /MediaBox [0 0 595 842] /Resources << /Font << /F1 ${font} 0 R >> >> /Contents ${contentRefs[i]} 0 R >>`}
 objs.push(`<< /Type /Pages /Kids [${pageRefs.map(x=>x+' 0 R').join(' ')}] /Count ${pageRefs.length} >>`);objs.push(`<< /Type /Catalog /Pages ${pagesObj} 0 R >>`);
 let out='%PDF-1.4\n',offs=[0];for(let i=0;i<objs.length;i++){offs.push(Buffer.byteLength(out));out+=`${i+1} 0 obj\n${objs[i]}\nendobj\n`}const xref=Buffer.byteLength(out);out+=`xref\n0 ${objs.length+1}\n0000000000 65535 f \n`;for(let i=1;i<=objs.length;i++)out+=String(offs[i]).padStart(10,'0')+' 00000 n \n';out+=`trailer\n<< /Size ${objs.length+1} /Root ${catalogObj} 0 R >>\nstartxref\n${xref}\n%%EOF`;fs.writeFileSync(file,out)
}


===== src\security.js =====

export function assertString(v,name,{max=255,min=1}={}){if(typeof v!=='string'||v.trim().length<min||v.length>max)throw Object.assign(new Error(`${name} invÃ¡lido`),{status:400});return v.trim()}
export function assertInt(v,name,{min=0,max=1000000000}={}){const n=Number(v);if(!Number.isInteger(n)||n<min||n>max)throw Object.assign(new Error(`${name} invÃ¡lido`),{status:400});return n}
export function assertMoney(v,name='Valor'){const n=Number(v);if(!Number.isFinite(n)||n<0||n>1000000000)throw Object.assign(new Error(`${name} invÃ¡lido`),{status:400});return Math.round(n*100)/100}
export function slugify(v){return String(v??'').normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').toLowerCase().trim().replace(/[^a-z0-9]+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'').slice(0,120)}


===== server.js =====

import http from 'node:http';import fs from 'node:fs';import path from 'node:path';import {URL} from 'node:url';
import {db,one,all,run,tx,id,audit,STORAGE} from './src/db.js';import {hashPassword,verifyPassword,sign,auth,requireRole} from './src/auth.js';import {makeOrderPdf} from './src/pdf.js';import {assertString,assertInt,assertMoney,slugify} from './src/security.js';
const PORT=Number(process.env.PORT||3000);const publicDir=path.join(process.cwd(),'public');
const IS_PROD=process.env.NODE_ENV==='production';
if(IS_PROD && (!process.env.JWT_SECRET || process.env.JWT_SECRET==='dev-xiquim-vargas-change-me')) throw new Error('JWT_SECRET obrigatÃ³rio e nÃ£o pode usar o segredo de desenvolvimento em produÃ§Ã£o');
const LOGIN_WINDOW_MS=60_000, LOGIN_MAX=10, loginAttempts=new Map();
const send=(res,status,data,headers={})=>{res.writeHead(status,{'content-type':'application/json; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store','x-content-type-options':'nosniff','x-frame-options':'DENY','referrer-policy':'no-referrer','permissions-policy':'camera=(self),geolocation=(self)','content-security-policy':"default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data: https:; connect-src 'self' https:; frame-ancestors 'none'",...headers});res.end(JSON.stringify(data))};
const html=(res,file)=>{res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':'text/html; charset=utf-8','cache-control':'no-store','x-content-type-options':'nosniff','x-frame-options':'DENY','referrer-policy':'no-referrer','content-security-policy':"default-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data: https:; connect-src 'self' https:; frame-ancestors 'none'"});res.end(fs.readFileSync(path.join(publicDir,file)))};
async function body(req){let d='',size=0;for await(const c of req){size+=c.length;if(size>2*1024*1024)throw Object.assign(new Error('Payload excede 8 MB'),{status:413});d+=c}if(!d)return{};try{const x=JSON.parse(d);if(!x||typeof x!=='object'||Array.isArray(x))throw new Error('Objeto JSON esperado');return x}catch{throw Object.assign(new Error('JSON invÃ¡lido'),{status:400})}}
function safeUser(u){return u&&{id:u.id,nome:u.nome,email:u.email,role:u.role,ativo:!!u.ativo,telefone:u.telefone}}
function priceFor(clienteId,produtoId){const p=one('SELECT * FROM produtos WHERE id=? AND ativo=1',[produtoId]);if(!p)throw Object.assign(new Error('Produto nÃ£o encontrado'),{status:404});const c=clienteId?one('SELECT tabela_preco_id FROM clientes WHERE id=?',[clienteId]):null;if(c?.tabela_preco_id){const x=one('SELECT preco FROM tabela_preco_itens WHERE tabela_id=? AND produto_id=?',[c.tabela_preco_id,produtoId]);if(x)return Number(x.preco)}return Number(p.preco_base)}
function visibleClient(user,clientId){if(user.role==='admin'||user.role==='cashier')return true;if(user.role==='seller')return !!one('SELECT 1 FROM clientes WHERE id=? AND vendedor_id=?',[clientId,user.sub]);if(user.role==='web_client')return !!one('SELECT 1 FROM usuario_clientes WHERE usuario_id=? AND cliente_id=?',[user.sub,clientId]);return false}
function visibleOrder(user,order){if(user.role==='admin'||user.role==='cashier')return true;if(user.role==='seller')return order.operador_id===user.sub||!!one('SELECT 1 FROM clientes WHERE id=? AND vendedor_id=?',[order.cliente_id,user.sub]);if(user.role==='web_client')return visibleClient(user,order.cliente_id);if(user.role==='driver')return !!one('SELECT 1 FROM rota_pedidos rp JOIN rotas_logistica r ON r.id=rp.rota_id WHERE rp.pedido_id=? AND r.motorista_id=?',[order.id,user.sub]);return false}
function createOrder(user,{cliente_id,itens,origem,observacoes}){if(!cliente_id||!Array.isArray(itens)||!itens.length)throw Object.assign(new Error('Cliente e itens sÃ£o obrigatÃ³rios'),{status:400});if(!visibleClient(user,cliente_id))throw Object.assign(new Error('Cliente fora da carteira'),{status:403});const allowed=['WEB','VENDEDOR','BALCAO'];if(!allowed.includes(origem))throw Object.assign(new Error('Origem invÃ¡lida'),{status:400});let result;tx(()=>{let total=0;const resolved=[];for(const item of itens){const p=one('SELECT * FROM produtos WHERE id=? AND ativo=1',[item.produto_id]);if(!p)throw Object.assign(new Error('Produto invÃ¡lido'),{status:400});const q=Number(item.quantidade);if(!Number.isInteger(q)||q<=0||q>100000)throw Object.assign(new Error('Quantidade invÃ¡lida'),{status:400});const st=one('SELECT * FROM estoque WHERE produto_id=?',[p.id]);if(!st||st.quantidade_disponivel<q)throw Object.assign(new Error(`Estoque insuficiente: ${p.nome}`),{status:409});const price=priceFor(cliente_id,p.id);total+=price*q;resolved.push({p,q,price,st})}const oid=id();const next=Number(one("SELECT COALESCE(MAX(CAST(substr(numero,9) AS INTEGER)),0)+1 n FROM pedidos WHERE numero LIKE 'XV-'||strftime('%Y','now')||'-%'")?.n||1);const numero='XV-'+new Date().getFullYear()+'-'+String(next).padStart(6,'0');run('INSERT INTO pedidos(id,numero,cliente_id,operador_id,origem,status,total,observacoes) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',[oid,numero,cliente_id,user.sub,origem,'PENDENTE',total,observacoes||null]);for(const x of resolved){run('INSERT INTO pedido_itens(id,pedido_id,produto_id,quantidade,preco_unitario_aplicado,subtotal) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)',[id(),oid,x.p.id,x.q,x.price,x.q*x.price]);run('UPDATE estoque SET quantidade_disponivel=quantidade_disponivel-?, quantidade_reservada=quantidade_reservada+?, updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE produto_id=? AND quantidade_disponivel>=?',[x.q,x.q,x.p.id,x.q]);run('INSERT INTO movimentacoes_estoque(id,produto_id,tipo,quantidade,quantidade_anterior,quantidade_posterior,operador_id,observacao) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?,?,?)',[id(),x.p.id,'RESERVA',-x.q,x.st.quantidade_disponivel,x.st.quantidade_disponivel-x.q,user.sub,`Reserva pedido ${numero}`])}const client=one('SELECT * FROM clientes WHERE id=?',[cliente_id]);const outstanding=Number(one("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total),0) n FROM pedidos WHERE cliente_id=? AND id<>? AND status<>'CONCLUIDO'",[cliente_id,oid])?.n||0);if(Number(client.limite_credito)>0 && outstanding+total>Number(client.limite_credito))throw Object.assign(new Error('Limite de crÃ©dito excedido'),{status:409});result=one('SELECT p.*,c.razao_social cliente_nome,c.cnpj_cpf FROM pedidos p JOIN clientes c ON c.id=p.cliente_id WHERE p.id=?',[oid]);audit({userId:user.sub,action:'CREATE_ORDER',entity:'pedido',entityId:oid,after:result})});
const items=all('SELECT pi.*,pr.nome produto_nome FROM pedido_itens pi JOIN produtos pr ON pr.id=pi.produto_id WHERE pi.pedido_id=?',[result.id]);result.origem_label={WEB:'Loja Online',VENDEDOR:'Vendedor Externo',BALCAO:'Frente de Loja'}[result.origem];const pdf=path.join(STORAGE,'pdfs',`${result.numero}.pdf`);try{makeOrderPdf(result,items,pdf);run('UPDATE pedidos SET pdf_path=?,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id=?',[pdf,result.id]);result.pdf_path=pdf}catch(e){console.error('PDF',e)}return result}
function listProducts(q=''){const like=`%${q}%`;return all(`SELECT p.*,c.nome categoria_nome,s.nome subcategoria_nome,COALESCE(e.quantidade_disponivel,0) quantidade_disponivel,COALESCE(e.quantidade_reservada,0) quantidade_reservada,COALESCE(e.estoque_minimo,0) estoque_minimo FROM produtos p LEFT JOIN categorias c ON c.id=p.categoria_id LEFT JOIN subcategorias s ON s.id=p.subcategoria_id LEFT JOIN estoque e ON e.produto_id=p.id WHERE p.ativo=1 AND (p.nome LIKE ? OR p.sku LIKE ? OR COALESCE(p.codigo_barras,'') LIKE ?) ORDER BY p.nome`,[like,like,like])}
async function route(req,res){const u=new URL(req.url,`http://${req.headers.host}`);const p=u.pathname;const method=req.method;
if(method==='GET'&&p==='/health')return send(res,200,{ok:true,service:'xiquim-vargas-erp',time:new Date().toISOString()});
if(method==='GET'&&p==='/')return html(res,'admin.html');
if(method==='GET'&&['/admin.html','/loja.html','/vendedor.html','/pdv.html','/motorista.html'].includes(p))return html(res,p.slice(1));
if(method==='GET'&&p.startsWith('/assets/')){const rel=p.replace('/assets/','');const f=path.resolve(publicDir,'assets',rel);if(!f.startsWith(path.resolve(publicDir,'assets')+path.sep)||!fs.existsSync(f))return send(res,404,{error:'Asset nÃ£o encontrado'});const ext=path.extname(f).toLowerCase();const types={'.jpg':'image/jpeg','.jpeg':'image/jpeg','.png':'image/png','.webp':'image/webp','.svg':'image/svg+xml','.css':'text/css','.js':'application/javascript'};res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':types[ext]||'application/octet-stream','cache-control':'public, max-age=86400','x-content-type-options':'nosniff'});return fs.createReadStream(f).pipe(res)}
if(method==='GET' && !p.startsWith('/api/') && !p.startsWith('/storage/') && p!=='/' && !p.startsWith('/assets/')){
const rel=decodeURIComponent(p.slice(1));
const f=path.resolve(publicDir,rel);
const base=path.resolve(publicDir);
if(!f.startsWith(base+path.sep)||!fs.existsSync(f)||!fs.statSync(f).isFile())return send(res,404,{error:'Arquivo não encontrado'});
const ext=path.extname(f).toLowerCase();
const types={
'.html':'text/html; charset=utf-8',
'.css':'text/css; charset=utf-8',
'.js':'application/javascript; charset=utf-8',
'.json':'application/json; charset=utf-8',
'.png':'image/png',
'.jpg':'image/jpeg',
'.jpeg':'image/jpeg',
'.webp':'image/webp',
'.svg':'image/svg+xml',
'.ico':'image/x-icon',
'.woff':'font/woff',
'.woff2':'font/woff2'
};
res.writeHead(200,{'content-type':types[ext]||'application/octet-stream','cache-control':'no-store','x-content-type-options':'nosniff'});
return fs.createReadStream(f).pipe(res)
}

1")?.valor);const depotLng=Number(b.depot_lng??one("SELECT valor FROM configuracoes WHERE chave IN ('deposito_longitude','depot.longitude') ORDER BY CASE chave WHEN 'deposito_longitude' THEN 0 ELSE 1 END LIMIT 1")?.valor);if(!Number.isFinite(depotLat)||!Number.isFinite(depotLng))throw Object.assign(new Error('Configure as coordenadas do depÃ³sito'),{status:409});let cur={latitude:depotLat,longitude:depotLng};while(rem.length){let bi=0,bd=Infinity;rem.forEach((x,i)=>{const d=Math.hypot(Number(x.latitude)-cur.latitude,Number(x.longitude)-cur.longitude);if(d<bd){bd=d;bi=i}});const x=rem.splice(bi,1)[0];seq.push(x);cur={latitude:Number(x.latitude),longitude:Number(x.longitude)}}ordered=[...seq,...orders.filter(x=>!points.includes(x))]}const rid=id();tx(()=>{run('INSERT INTO rotas_logistica(id,motorista_id,data,status,sequencia_json) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?)',[rid,b.motorista_id||null,new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10),'PLANEJADA',JSON.stringify(ordered.map(x=>x.id))]);ordered.forEach((o,i)=>{run('INSERT INTO rota_pedidos(rota_id,pedido_id,ordem) VALUES(?,?,?)',[rid,o.id,i+1]);run("UPDATE pedidos SET status='ROTA',updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id=? AND status='SEPARACAO'",[o.id])});audit({userId:user.sub,action:'CREATE_ROUTE',entity:'rota',entityId:rid,after:{ids:ordered.map(x=>x.id)}})});return send(res,201,{item:one('SELECT * FROM rotas_logistica WHERE id=?',[rid]),ordered:ordered.map((x,i)=>({ordem:i+1,...x}))})}
if(method==='GET'&&p==='/api/logistics/routes'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin','driver']);let rows=all(`SELECT r.*,u.nome motorista_nome FROM rotas_logistica r LEFT JOIN usuarios u ON u.id=r.motorista_id ORDER BY r.data DESC,r.created_at DESC`);if(user.role==='driver')rows=rows.filter(x=>x.motorista_id===user.sub);return send(res,200,{items:rows})}
if(method==='POST'&&p.startsWith('/api/logistics/orders/')&&p.endsWith('/complete')){const user=requireRole(req,['driver','admin']);const oid=p.split('/')[4];const order=one('SELECT * FROM pedidos WHERE id=?',[oid]);if(!order||!visibleOrder(user,order))throw Object.assign(new Error('Pedido nÃ£o encontrado'),{status:404});if(order.status!=='ROTA')throw Object.assign(new Error('Somente pedidos em ROTA podem ser concluÃ­dos'),{status:409});tx(()=>{const changed=run("UPDATE pedidos SET status='CONCLUIDO',updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id=? AND status='ROTA'",[oid]);if(changed.changes!==1)throw Object.assign(new Error('Pedido jÃ¡ foi concluÃ­do por outro operador'),{status:409});for(const it of all('SELECT produto_id,quantidade FROM pedido_itens WHERE pedido_id=?',[oid])) run('UPDATE estoque SET quantidade_reservada=MAX(0,quantidade_reservada-?),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE produto_id=?',[it.quantidade,it.produto_id]);audit({userId:user.sub,action:'DELIVERY_COMPLETE',entity:'pedido',entityId:oid,before:order,after:one('SELECT * FROM pedidos WHERE id=?',[oid])})});return send(res,200,{item:one('SELECT * FROM pedidos WHERE id=?',[oid])})}
if(method==='POST'&&p.startsWith('/api/logistics/orders/')&&p.endsWith('/proof')){const user=requireRole(req,['driver','admin']);const oid=p.split('/')[4];const order=one('SELECT * FROM pedidos WHERE id=?',[oid]);if(!order||!visibleOrder(user,order))throw Object.assign(new Error('Pedido nÃ£o encontrado'),{status:404});const b=await body(req);if(order.status!=='ROTA')throw Object.assign(new Error('Comprovante sÃ³ pode ser anexado em ROTA'),{status:409});if(!b.data_url?.startsWith('data:image/'))throw Object.assign(new Error('Envie uma imagem em data URL'),{status:400});const m=b.data_url.match(/^data:image\/(png|jpeg|jpg);base64,(.+)$/);if(!m)throw Object.assign(new Error('Formato invÃ¡lido'),{status:400});const raw=Buffer.from(m[2],'base64');if(raw.length>5*1024*1024)throw Object.assign(new Error('Comprovante excede 5 MB'),{status:413});const file=path.join(STORAGE,'proofs',`${oid}-${Date.now()}.${m[1]==='jpeg'?'jpg':m[1]}`);fs.writeFileSync(file,raw);const cid=id();run('INSERT INTO comprovantes_entrega(id,pedido_id,motorista_id,arquivo_path) VALUES(?,?,?,?)',[cid,oid,user.sub,file]);audit({userId:user.sub,action:'UPLOAD_PROOF',entity:'pedido',entityId:oid,after:{file}});return send(res,201,{ok:true,path:`/storage/proofs/${path.basename(file)}`})}
if(method==='GET'&&p==='/api/audit'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);return send(res,200,{items:all(`SELECT a.*,u.nome user_nome FROM audit_logs a LEFT JOIN usuarios u ON u.id=a.user_id ORDER BY a.created_at DESC LIMIT 300`)})}
if(method==='GET'&&p==='/api/users'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);return send(res,200,{items:all('SELECT id,nome,email,role,ativo,telefone,created_at FROM usuarios ORDER BY nome')})}
if(method==='PUT'&&p.startsWith('/api/users/')){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);const uid=p.split('/')[3];const before=one('SELECT * FROM usuarios WHERE id=?',[uid]);if(!before)throw Object.assign(new Error('UsuÃ¡rio nÃ£o encontrado'),{status:404});const b=await body(req);const roles=['admin','seller','cashier','driver','web_client'];if(b.role&&!roles.includes(b.role))throw Object.assign(new Error('Role invÃ¡lida'),{status:400});if(uid===user.sub&&b.ativo===false)throw Object.assign(new Error('NÃ£o Ã© possÃ­vel desativar o prÃ³prio usuÃ¡rio'),{status:409});run('UPDATE usuarios SET nome=COALESCE(?,nome),email=COALESCE(?,email),role=COALESCE(?,role),telefone=?,ativo=COALESCE(?,ativo),senha_hash=COALESCE(?,senha_hash),updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP WHERE id=?',[b.nome,b.email?String(b.email).toLowerCase():null,b.role,b.telefone??before.telefone,b.ativo,b.password?hashPassword(String(b.password)):null,uid]);const after=one('SELECT id,nome,email,role,ativo,telefone,updated_at FROM usuarios WHERE id=?',[uid]);audit({userId:user.sub,action:'UPDATE_USER',entity:'usuario',entityId:uid,before,after});return send(res,200,{item:after})}
if(method==='POST'&&p==='/api/users'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);const b=await body(req);const uid=id();run('INSERT INTO usuarios(id,nome,email,senha_hash,role,telefone) VALUES(?,?,?,?,?,?)',[uid,b.nome,String(b.email).toLowerCase(),hashPassword(b.password||'ChangeMe123!'),b.role,b.telefone||null]);audit({userId:user.sub,action:'CREATE_USER',entity:'usuario',entityId:uid,after:{...b,password:undefined}});return send(res,201,{item:one('SELECT id,nome,email,role,ativo FROM usuarios WHERE id=?',[uid])})}
if(method==='GET'&&p==='/api/settings'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);return send(res,200,{items:all('SELECT * FROM configuracoes ORDER BY chave')})}
if(method==='POST'&&p==='/api/settings'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);const b=await body(req);run('INSERT INTO configuracoes(chave,valor) VALUES(?,?) ON CONFLICT(chave) DO UPDATE SET valor=excluded.valor,updated_at=CURRENT_TIMESTAMP',[b.chave,String(b.valor)]);audit({userId:user.sub,action:'UPDATE_SETTING',entity:'configuracao',entityId:b.chave,after:b});return send(res,200,{item:one('SELECT * FROM configuracoes WHERE chave=?',[b.chave])})}
if(method==='GET'&&p==='/api/reports'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);return send(res,200,{origens:all('SELECT origem,COUNT(*) pedidos,COALESCE(SUM(total),0) faturamento FROM pedidos WHERE status=\'CONCLUIDO\' GROUP BY origem'),topProdutos:all(`SELECT pr.nome,SUM(pi.quantidade) quantidade,ROUND(SUM(pi.subtotal),2) faturamento FROM pedido_itens pi JOIN pedidos p ON p.id=pi.pedido_id JOIN produtos pr ON pr.id=pi.produto_id WHERE p.status='CONCLUIDO' GROUP BY pr.id ORDER BY quantidade DESC LIMIT 20`),vendedores:all(`SELECT u.nome,COUNT(p.id) pedidos,COALESCE(SUM(p.total),0) faturamento FROM usuarios u LEFT JOIN pedidos p ON p.operador_id=u.id AND p.status='CONCLUIDO' WHERE u.role='seller' GROUP BY u.id`)})}
if(method==='POST'&&p==='/api/admin/impersonate'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);const b=await body(req);const target=one('SELECT * FROM usuarios WHERE id=? AND ativo=1',[b.user_id]);if(!target)throw Object.assign(new Error('UsuÃ¡rio nÃ£o encontrado'),{status:404});audit({userId:user.sub,action:'IMPERSONATE_START',entity:'usuario',entityId:target.id,after:{effectiveUserId:target.id}});return send(res,200,{token:sign({sub:target.id,role:target.role,email:target.email,nome:target.nome,actor_user_id:user.sub,impersonation:true},3600),user:safeUser(target),impersonation:true,actor_user_id:user.sub})}
if(method==='POST'&&p==='/api/admin/ia'){const user=requireRole(req,['admin']);const b=await body(req);const stats={estoqueCritico:one("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM estoque WHERE quantidade_disponivel<=estoque_minimo")?.n||0,pedidosPendentes:one("SELECT COUNT(*) n FROM pedidos WHERE status='PENDENTE'")?.n||0,faturamentoConcluido:one("SELECT COALESCE(SUM(total),0) n FROM pedidos WHERE status='CONCLUIDO'")?.n||0};return send(res,200,{answer:`Contexto determinÃ­stico do ERP: ${stats.estoqueCritico} produtos em estoque crÃ­tico, ${stats.pedidosPendentes} pedidos pendentes e R$ ${Number(stats.faturamentoConcluido).toFixed(2)} faturados em pedidos concluÃ­dos. Pergunta recebida: ${String(b.message||'')}`})}
return send(res,404,{error:'Rota nÃ£o encontrada'});
}catch(e){console.error(e);return send(res,e.status||500,{error:e.message||'Erro interno'})}}
const server=http.createServer((req,res)=>{Promise.resolve(route(req,res)).catch(e=>{if(!res.headersSent)send(res,e.status||500,{error:e.message||'Erro interno'});else res.destroy()})});server.listen(PORT,()=>console.log(`Xiquim Vargas ERP em http://localhost:${PORT}`));
